    Shortcut: READMASSCHARGE

    Module generic
    Description Usage
    Set the masses and charges from an input PDB file.

    Details and examples

    Set the masses and charges from an input PDB file.

    You can use this command to read in the masses and charges for the atoms as shown in the example input below:

    Click on the labels of the actions for more information on what each action computes 
    #SETTINGS INPUTFILES=regtest/basic/rt-readmasscharge2/mcinpt
mqThe READMASSCHARGE action with label mq calculates the following quantities:
     Quantity    Type    Description  
    mq_massvectorthe masses of the atoms in the system
    mq_chargesvectorthe masses of the atoms in the system
    : READMASSCHARGESet the masses and charges from an input PDB file. This action is a shortcut. More details FILEinput file that contains the masses and charges that should be used=
    regtest/basic/rt-readmasscharge2/mcinpt
     Click here to see an extract from this file.

The READMASSCHARGE action with label mq calculates the following quantities:
     Quantity    Description  
    mq.massthe masses of the atoms in the system
    mq.chargesthe masses of the atoms in the system
    mq_massThe CONSTANT action with label mq_mass calculates the following quantities:

# --- End of included input ---

    In the above example the masses and charges are in a file called mcinpt that contains three columns of numbers. The first column of numbers is a numerical index, the second column is the masses and the third is the charges.

    You can read masses and charges from a PDB file instead by using an input like the one shown below:

    Click on the labels of the actions for more information on what each action computes 
    #SETTINGS NATOMS=108 INPUTFILES=regtest/basic/rt-readmasscharge/test.pdb

     Quantity    Type    Description

    In this chages masses are read from the occupancy column and charges are read from the beta column.

    Using this command to read in masses and charges is useful when you are postprocessing a trajectory using driver. If you are using PLUMED as a plugin to a molecular dynamics code the masses and charges will often be passed from the MD code to PLUMED.

    Output components

    This action calculates the values in the following table. These values can be referenced elsewhere in the input by using this Action's label followed by a dot and the name of the value required from the list below.

    Name Type Description
    mass vector the masses of the atoms in the system
    charges vector the masses of the atoms in the system

    Full list of keywords

    The following table describes the keywords and options that can be used with this action

    Keyword Type Default Description
    PDBFILE compulsory none a pdb file that contains the masses and charges of the atoms in the beta and occupancy columns
    FILE optional not used input file that contains the masses and charges that should be used
